## Phase 4 Task 4.5：Spot heal 的 Mac UI (2026-09-04, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`（自動化部分；GUI 自動化操作驗測本輪未重跑，見下方 `NOT RUN`）。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，base 仍是 `main@fb7109a`，這一輪之前的 HEAD 是 `9436f38`（Task 4.4 文件 commit）。Product+test commit：`1f3ee74`。開始前確認過分支/HEAD/dirty files 與 `CURRENT.md`/`AGENTS.md`/roadmap 描述的狀態一致，worktree 乾淨。嚴格只做 roadmap Task 4.5 範圍——spot heal 的 Mac UI（新增/選取/移動 source/移動 target/大小/羽化/刪除/模式切換的 source-contract，加上誠實記錄品質限制）；沒有補跑 A11 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z`（仍是 `NOT RUN`）；沒有重跑一次 Task 4.3 當時做過的完整自動化 GUI 操作驗測流程（build app bundle + `osascript`/`CGEvent`），這輪的手動清單新增段落（B1–B13）全部誠實列為 `NOT RUN`，理由見下方。
- **UI 結構**：延續 `LocalAdjustmentsPanel`（不新開一個面板）——在既有「Add Gradient / 漸層清單」下方加一個平行的「Add Spot Heal / 修護清單」區塊，兩段共用同一份「Local adjustments are non-destructive.」頁尾文字。新增 `Sources/LumaHarborApp/Views/SpotHealOverlayView.swift`（照 `LinearGradientOverlayView` 的「Inspector 面板 + 畫布拖曳 overlay」兩層架構複製一份）：每個 `.spotHeal` entry 三個獨立把手——**target**（拖曳移動修護目標點）、**size**（固定方位在 target 右側，拖曳改變 `radius`）、**source**（僅 `.clone` 模式顯示，拖曳移動取樣來源點）。`EditorToolMode` 新增 `.spotHeal`；沒有新增 `EditorSession` 狀態欄位——`selectedLocalAdjustmentID` 本來就是 kind-agnostic 的共用選取狀態，Task 4.1 時就已經這樣設計。所有編輯都走 `editor.updateAdjustments(_:)`，跟其他每一個調整控制項共用同一條 undo/autosave 路徑。
- **拖曳數學抽成純函式，可獨立單元測試**：新增 `Sources/LumaHarborApp/Views/SpotHealDragMath.swift`，比照 `LinearGradientDragMath` 當初的理由拆出來。`updatedTargetPosition`/`updatedSourcePosition` 跟漸層的 `updatedPosition` 幾乎一樣（只改 x/y 或 sourceX/sourceY，靠 `LocalAdjustmentGeometry` 自己的 `didSet` clamp）；`updatedRadius` 是新的一種正規化方式——用「target 到 size 把手的直線距離」除以 `min(imageFrameSize.width, imageFrameSize.height)`，刻意選這個除數而不是像漸層 `range` 那樣除以對角線的一半，因為這正是 `LocalAdjustmentRenderer.applySpotHeal` 自己算 `radiusPixels` 用的除數（`min(extent.width, extent.height)`）——選錯的話畫面上圓形把手的大小會跟實際套用的修護範圍對不起來。
- **`resolvedSource(for:)`——刻意複製 render 層 `autoSourcePoint` 的公式，不是共用**：`.clone` 模式尚未放置來源點時，overlay 需要知道來源把手該畫在哪裡（不能畫在「不存在」的地方），公式跟 `LocalAdjustmentRenderer.autoSourcePoint(for:extent:)`（target 正上方 `radius × 2.5`，太靠上緣時鏡射到下方）完全一致，但是獨立在 `SpotHealDragMath` 裡重新寫一份，而不是讓 `LumaHarborApp` 想辦法呼叫 `RawProcessingCore` 裡那個 `private` 函式——兩個模組之間沒有第三方可以共同依賴而不倒轉 render pipeline 自己的分層。用 `SpotHealDragMathTests` 把兩份公式的數值結果釘死一致，確保之後任一邊改了公式而另一邊沒跟著改，會在這裡先被抓到，而不是等到「overlay 畫的把手位置」跟「實際 render 出來的效果」肉眼看起來對不上時才發現。只有 `sourceX`/`sourceY` 兩個欄位都有值才算「已放置」，其中一個有值另一個沒有（schema 本身不會產生，但手改 sidecar 有可能）視同兩個都沒有。
- **`.heal` 模式刻意不顯示可拖曳的來源把手**：design spec §6.7 的 `.heal` 定義是「自動取樣周邊材質」，沒有使用者放置的來源點這回事；如果在 `.heal` 模式下也畫一個可拖曳的來源把手，會誤導使用者以為拖它有效果（實際上 render 完全忽略 `.heal` 模式的 `sourceX`/`sourceY`，這是 Task 4.4 就已經釘住的行為）。這個範圍界線寫在 `SpotHealOverlayView` 自己的 header 註解與 `SpotHealOverlayContractTests.testOverlayOnlyOffersTheSourceHandleInCloneMode` 裡。
- **品質限制誠實記錄（roadmap Task 4.5 明確要求）**：`LocalAdjustmentsPanel` 在選取中的修護點是 `.heal` 模式時，額外顯示兩行說明文字（「「修復」模式會自動取樣周邊材質。」/「在複雜背景上，改用「仿製」並自行指定來源點會更可靠。」），切到 `.clone` 後這兩行消失——不只是 render 層程式碼註解裡講品質限制，UI 上也讓使用者在操作當下就看到，`SpotHealOverlayContractTests.testPanelRecordsHealModesQualityLimitationHonestly` 釘住這個字串存在。
- **面板控制項**：新增修護點的按鈕（`kind: .spotHeal`，其餘用 `LocalAdjustment`/`LocalAdjustmentGeometry` 的預設值，沒有像漸層那樣預設一個非 identity 的 patch，因為 spot heal 完全不使用 `.adjustments`——Task 4.4 就已經記錄這個範圍界線）；每列有 enabled 開關、刪除按鈕（重用 `Array<LocalAdjustment>.removing(_:)`）；選取後顯示 `Picker`（Heal/Clone，用 `SpotHealMode` 本身而非另外發明一個 Bool）、`Radius` 滑桿（`0.01...0.3`，比漸層 exposure 的 `-5...5` 窄很多，因為 `radius` 的 schema 全域範圍是 `[0,1]` 但 `1.0` 代表半徑等於圖片短邊的一半，對修護筆刷來說是不合理的極端值——滑桿範圍是 UI 層的合理化，不是 schema 的限制，拖曳把手仍然可以透過 `SpotHealDragMath.updatedRadius` 走到 schema 允許的任何值，只是滑桿本身不鼓勵）、`Feather` 滑桿（沿用 `LocalAdjustmentGeometry.featherRange` 也就是 `0...100`，跟漸層共用同一個常數來源，不是各自硬編碼一份）。兩個滑桿的 reset 都指回 `LocalAdjustmentGeometry.neutral` 的對應欄位值，而不是像漸層 exposure 那樣重設成 `nil`——`radius`/`feather` 是非 optional 欄位，沒有「未設定」這個狀態可以退回。
- **TDD**：先寫 `SpotHealDragMathTests.swift`（15 個案例：target/size/source 三種拖曳各自的正規化與互不干擾、`resolvedSource` 的預設偏移與鏡射邊界情況）跟 `SpotHealOverlayContractTests.swift`（15 個案例，比照 `LinearGradientOverlayContractTests` 的原始碼字串比對手法——overlay 掛載條件、`AspectFitRect.fitting` 呼叫次數、三種拖曳手勢各自呼叫對應的 `SpotHealDragMath` 純函式、`.clone` 才顯示 source handle、accessibility label、面板 add/delete/mode-switch/radius/feather/品質限制文字），先跑 `swift test --filter` 確認是真的因為 `SpotHealDragMath`/`SpotHealOverlayView` 不存在而編譯失敗（不是只看 IDE 提示），才動手實作。`LocalizationSmokeTest` 也先新增 `testEverySpotHealStringHasAChineseTranslation`（14 個新 key）確認 RED，再把兩份 `.lproj` 補上翻譯轉綠。
- **在地化**：面板/overlay 新增的 14 個字串已經加進 en/zh-Hant 兩份 `.lproj`（`Radius`/`Feather`/`Enabled`/`Done` 重用 Task 4.3 既有 key，沒有重複宣告）。
- **手動 screenshot checklist（roadmap 自己要求的 Task 4.5 交付項）**：`docs/testing/beta/PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 新增「B. 局部修護拖曳把手（Task 4.5）」段落，B1–B13 涵蓋新增/移動 target/移動 size/切換 heal↔clone 立即顯示或隱藏 source 把手/移動 source/Radius-Feather 滑桿/品質限制文字/刪除/停用/多點獨立性/離開編輯模式/undo/重新開啟持久化/匯出反映效果。**全部 13 項誠實列為 `NOT RUN`**——這輪只完成程式碼與自動化測試，沒有重跑一次 Task 4.3 當時做過的那種完整 GUI 自動化操作流程（build app bundle、隔離測試環境、`osascript`/自製 `CGEvent` 工具逐項操作 + sidecar JSON 讀值佐證），留給下一輪或真人補跑；同時把停止條件段落（原「B. 停止條件」改編號為「C.」）加了一條「切換 heal/clone 模式後畫面沒有立即反映」對應 design spec §6.7 的要求。
- **驗證**：聚焦測試（`SpotHealDragMathTests|SpotHealOverlayContractTests|LocalizationSmokeTest|LinearGradient*|LocalAdjustment*|PhotoExportTests`）127 執行、0 失敗。完整 `swift test`：**1603 執行**（比上一輪基準 1572 多 31——`SpotHealDragMathTests` 15、`SpotHealOverlayContractTests` 15、`LocalizationSmokeTest` 1）、9 skip（既有 `RawFixtureTests` 基準不變）、0 失敗。`swift build` 乾淨。iOS generic build（`xcodebuild -project Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj -scheme LumaHarborPad -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build`，因為 `EditorCore`/`AdjustmentUI` 是共用 library product）——`** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`，`project.pbxproj` 的 md5 建置前後確認完全相同（`308082ad4cfcbbbe25693d87440e7f3d`，跟上一輪記錄的基準值一致，未被讀取或變動）。`git diff --check` 乾淨。隱私掃描（`rg -n "/Users/|/Volumes/|/private/|<TEAM_ID>|teamIdentifier:|DEVELOPMENT_TEAM"` 對這輪 product/test/docs diff）零命中。
- **`NOT RUN`**：`PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 的 B1–B13（本輪沒有重跑 GUI 自動化操作流程，理由見上）；A11 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z`（Phase 4 完整驗收／上線候選 gate，這輪沒有嘗試補跑，也不阻擋這輪的開發範圍，延續 Task 4.3 那輪的既有狀態）。
- **Next action**：(a) 補跑 `PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` B1–B13（可比照 A 段當時用過的 `osascript`/`CGEvent` GUI 自動化方式，或由真人操作，逐項留下 sidecar JSON 讀值或截圖佐證），(b) 補跑 A11 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z`，(c) 完成以上兩項後進入 Task 4.6（Phase 4 完整驗證輪：focused tests/`swift test`/`git diff --check`/隱私掃描彙整 + Mac 手動清單 screenshots）。未經使用者明確授權，不 push、不 merge、不 rebase、不移除 worktree、不刪分支。

## Phase 4 Task 4.4：Spot heal / clone model and render (2026-09-04, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，base 仍是 `main@fb7109a`，這一輪之前的 HEAD 是 `d300aa9`（Task 4.3 手動驗測清單審查修正的文件 commit）。Product+test commit：`037471c`。開始前確認過分支/HEAD/dirty files 與 `CURRENT.md`/`AGENTS.md`/roadmap/`PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 描述的狀態一致，worktree 乾淨。嚴格只做 roadmap Task 4.4 範圍——target/source movement 測試、mode switch 立即更新選取點測試、full-resolution export 測試、保守的 Core Image heal/clone 實作。沒有做 Task 4.5（spot heal 的 Mac UI）；沒有把 A11 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z` 改成 `PASS`（仍是 `NOT RUN`，這輪完全沒碰手動驗測清單本身）。
- **Render 實作**：`LocalAdjustmentRenderer.apply(_:to:)` 從「只處理 `.linearGradient`、跳過 `.spotHeal`」（Task 4.2 的既有範圍界線）改成兩種 kind 都處理，用 `switch adjustment.kind` 分派。新增 `applySpotHeal(_:to:)`：把整張圖依 `target - source` 位移量整個平移一份，再用一個以 target 為中心、`CIRadialGradient` 做出的白色圓形柔邊遮罩（`healMask`），把平移後的圖跟原圖 `CIBlendWithMask` 混合——效果是「把 source 附近的內容貼到 target」，跟 `applyLinearGradient` 共用同一個新抽出的 `imagePoint(x:y:extent:)` 座標轉換 helper（原本內嵌在 `linearGradientMask` 裡的 anchor 換算邏輯，這輪抽成兩邊共用）。`healMask` 的 feather 換算方式仿照 `AdjustmentPipeline.applyVignette` 既有的 `featherDistance`/`innerRadius` 慣例（`feather=0` 給一個很窄的固定寬度過渡帶而非真的零寬度，避開 `radius0==radius1` 的退化情況；`feather=100` 讓整個半徑範圍都在漸變）。
- **`resolvedSourcePoint(for:extent:)`——mode switch 立即生效的關鍵**：每次 render 呼叫都重新從 `geometry` 讀值，不快取、不記憶前一次呼叫的狀態。`.clone` 模式若有 `sourceX`/`sourceY` 就直接用；`.heal` 模式**永遠忽略** `sourceX`/`sourceY`（即使是從先前 `.clone` 選取殘留下來的值），改用 `autoSourcePoint(for:extent:)` 的固定 offset 自動取樣。這代表只翻轉同一個 `LocalAdjustment` 的 `healMode` 欄位、其他欄位（包含已經放置的 source 點）完全不動，render 結果就會立即改變——對應 design spec §6.7「模式切換時，當前選取點必須立即更新，不只影響下一個新點」在 render 層的實踐，`LocalAdjustmentRendererTests.testSwitchingHealModeOnTheSameAdjustmentImmediatelyChangesBehaviorWithoutClearingTheSourcePoint` 專門釘住這個行為。
- **`autoSourcePoint`（heal 模式的「自動取樣周邊材質」，design spec §6.7）誠實記錄品質限制**：實作是保守、決定性的固定 offset——從 target 正上方 `radius × 2.5`（normalized，上限 0.45）取樣，太靠近上緣時鏡射到下方，不是 content-aware fill，也不是任何學習或分析過的選擇。程式碼註解與這則記錄都明講：在較單調的區域附近效果還可以，但在複雜材質或高對比邊界上可能會明顯重複貼上不相關的內容；`.clone` 模式配合使用者自己放的 source 點才是可靠路徑。沒有承諾 AI 修圖品質，符合 roadmap Phase 4 Goal 原文「先做...不追求 AI 修圖」與使用者這輪的明確要求。
- **`.spotHeal` 不使用 `.adjustments`（局部 mini adjustment patch）**：design spec §6.7 沒有像 §6.6 對線性漸層那樣列出 spot heal 的 mini adjustment 欄位；heal/clone 是內容操作、不是色調操作，所以這輪的 render 完全不理會 `.spotHeal` entry 上的 `.adjustments` 欄位（schema 本身仍是共用欄位，只是 render 這層選擇不用它），已在程式碼註解與這則記錄裡明講這個範圍界線。
- **TDD**：先寫 `LocalAdjustmentRendererTests.swift` 的新測試（target/source movement、mode switch 立即生效、disabled/no-op/no-source 邊界情況）、`LocalAdjustmentTests.swift` 新增的 `LocalAdjustmentSpotHealModelTests`（純模型層的 target/source 獨立移動、mode switch 不動到其他欄位/其他 entry，不牽涉 Core Image）、`PhotoExportTests.swift` 的兩個 full-resolution export 測試，跑 `swift test --filter` 確認真的是 RED（5 個 render 斷言失敗、1 個 export 斷言失敗，其餘因為欄位/型別本來就存在所以編譯沒問題、行為斷言真的沒過），才動手實作 render。舊測試 `testSpotHealEntriesAreIgnoredByThisRenderer`（Task 4.2 自己記錄的暫時範圍界線，早就寫明「這是 Task 4.4 的工作」）已被移除，換成這輪一整組新的 spot heal 測試。
- **合成 fixture 選擇**：新增 `LocalAdjustmentRendererTests.makeHalvesImage`（左半純藍、右半純紅）取代 `makeFlatImage`——clone/heal 需要 source 跟 target 之間有真正的內容差異，才能斷言「內容真的被搬過去」，純色 fixture 會讓 clone/heal 的效果完全看不出來。水平分割不需要像 `GeometryRendererTests.makeQuadrantImage` 那樣做視覺座標到 Core Image y-up 的翻轉（x 軸在兩種座標系裡意義相同）。`PhotoExportTests.SyntheticRawDecoder` 新增 `contentImage: CIImage?` 覆寫欄位（預設仍是既有的純色 0.4/0.5/0.6，所有既有測試不受影響），export 測試用同款左藍右紅內容取代預設純色，才能驗到匯出檔案真的有搬動內容而不是只有亮度變化。
- **驗證**：聚焦測試（`LocalAdjustment.*|PhotoExportTests`）102 執行、0 失敗。完整 `swift test`：**1572 執行**（比上一輪基準 1559 多 13——render 6、export 2、model 5）、9 skip（既有 `RawFixtureTests` 基準不變）、0 失敗。`swift build` 乾淨。iOS generic build（`xcodebuild -project Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj -scheme LumaHarborPad -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build`，因為 `RawProcessingCore`/`AdjustmentUI` 是共用 module）——`** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`，`project.pbxproj` 的 md5 建置前後確認完全相同（`308082ad4cfcbbbe25693d87440e7f3d`），未被讀取或變動。`git diff --check` 乾淨。隱私掃描（`rg -n "/Users/|/Volumes/|/private/|<TEAM_ID>|teamIdentifier:|DEVELOPMENT_TEAM"` 對這輪 product/test diff）零命中。
- **`NOT RUN`**：Task 4.5（spot heal 的 Mac UI）不在這輪範圍內，不是遺漏——這輪完全是 model/render 層的自動化測試，沒有走到 UI，也沒有新增手動驗測項目（`PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 目前仍只涵蓋 Task 4.3 的線性漸層拖曳把手，等 Task 4.5 做完 UI 後才有畫面可以人眼確認 heal/clone 的實際效果）。A11 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z` 依然是 `NOT RUN`（Phase 4 完整驗收／上線候選 gate，這輪沒有嘗試補跑，也不阻擋這輪的開發範圍）。
- **Next action**：Task 4.5（Spot heal UI）——roadmap 自己要求 UI source-contracts（新增、選取、移動 source、移動 target、大小、羽化、刪除、mode switch）並誠實記錄品質限制；`LocalAdjustmentsPanel.swift`/`LinearGradientOverlayView.swift`/`LinearGradientDragMath.swift` 這組 Task 4.3 已經建好的「Inspector 面板 + 畫布拖曳 overlay + 純函式拖曳數學」架構可以直接照抄一份給 spot heal。或者先讓真人補跑 A11 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z` 與 `PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 其餘項目。未經使用者明確授權，不 push、不 merge、不 rebase、不移除 worktree、不刪分支。

## Phase 4 Task 4.3 手動驗測審查修正：PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md A11 實體鍵盤 ⌘Z 尚未完成 (2026-09-04, Codex, docs-only review, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`PARTIAL PASS`（手動驗測文件修正）。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，worktree `.worktrees/claude-awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，base 仍是 `main@fb7109a`。Codex 審查 `f20af0a` 後確認它是 docs-only commit，沒有 product code 變更；但該 commit 把 A11 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z` 誤標為 `PASS`，且新增了未去識別化的本機絕對路徑。這輪修正文件，不改 product code。
- **驗測方式（去識別化後記錄）**：沒有真人手動操作，而是由 Claude 透過 `osascript`/System Events 存取樹查詢＋點擊，加上現場編譯的 `CGEvent`-based 命令列小工具（`click`/`drag`/`scroll`）對真實執行中的 `build/LumaHarbor.app`（`Scripts/build-app-bundle.sh debug`，隔離 home 代號 `ISOLATED-HOME-001`，照片庫代號 `APFS-TMP-001`）送出真實滑鼠/鍵盤事件；每一項的判定依據都是 sidecar JSON 逐欄位讀值，加上截圖比對，不是只看畫面「感覺對不對」。詳細每一項的操作與讀值記在 `PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 本身，這裡只記重點與跟未來工作有關的發現。
- **A1–A9（拖曳把手）**：全部 PASS。新增漸層／拖曳位置／拖曳方向到 0°/90°／拖近拖遠改變 range／曝光滑桿局部生效／刪除只影響單一漸層／停用後效果消失但把手與拖曳仍可用／兩個漸層互相獨立且點列可切換選取，逐項都有 sidecar 數值變化佐證。方向慣例（往下拖＝角度變正、非负）跟 `LinearGradientDragMathTests` 的既有單元測試結論一致，這次是拿真實視窗座標再驗證一次，沒有發現落差。
- **A10/A12/A13（離開編輯模式／持久性／匯出）**：PASS。A12 特地測了最嚴格版本——從選單完整 Quit App、用同樣隔離環境重開、重新雙擊照片，確認漸層的位置/方向/範圍/曝光/啟用狀態逐欄位跟關閉前相同。A13 特地走真人會用的「File › 匯出 JPEG…」UI 流程（不是呼叫測試內部 API），匯出後用獨立寫的 `CGImageSource` 像素讀取工具比對「有漸層」跟「沒漸層」兩份匯出檔案，證實局部曝光效果確實寫進了匯出檔案，且只影響漸層範圍內的像素、範圍外像素逐 bit 相同。
- **A11（Undo）**：`NOT RUN` for 實體鍵盤 `⌘Z`。Claude 的記錄顯示合成 `⌘Z` 沒有被 App 收到、undo 沒有發生；Codex 這輪用同一份隔離資料補試 `super+z`、`cmd+z`、`Command+z` 也都沒有讓 local adjustment 的 `isEnabled=false` 復原。改用滑鼠點工具列 Undo 後，sidecar JSON 與畫面都有正確復原。這只能證明選單／工具列 Undo 可用，不能證明 A11 原本要求的實體鍵盤 `⌘Z` 已通過。`PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 已改成 `PARTIAL PASS`，A11 維持 `NOT RUN`，等待真人實體鍵盤或另一個確實能產生等價硬體鍵盤事件的驗測方式補跑。
- **這輪發現、但不算 Phase 4 迴歸的既有限制（已寫進 checklist 的整體結論，供下次驗測或 spot heal UI 開發時參考）**：
  1. 合成的 `⌘Z` `CGEvent`/`System Events keystroke` 按鍵事件無法穩定觸發 App 的 Undo——`Sources/LumaHarborApp/LumaHarborMainApp.swift` 的 `UndoRedoKeyEquivalentFix` 用 local key-down monitor 接鍵盤事件，這次合成事件沒被接住；改用滑鼠點「編輯 › 復原」選單就正常。純自動化環境目前不能把這條路徑標為 PASS。
  2. App 重啟後 Inspector 面板預設捲到最上方（直方圖/詮釋資料/Preset），不記得使用者上次捲到「局部調整」的位置——是既有的面板行為，不是這輪新增的迴歸，但容易讓下一個測試的人誤以為漸層不見了，值得在 spot heal UI 也共用同一個面板時留意。
  3. 測試相片（`phase3-a-renamed.ARW`）基礎曝光偏高，套用較大的局部曝光值時大範圍會直接裁到 255,255,255，此時像素比對看不出差異；A13 改用較低曝光值＋刻意挑選漸層範圍內外的取樣點才量到有意義的差異。之後如果要建立自動化的「匯出像素回歸測試」用這張照片，要注意這個 clipping 陷阱。
- **下一步**：A11 的實體鍵盤 `⌘Z` 是 Phase 4 完整驗收／上線候選 gate；補跑可通過後，再把 checklist 改回 full PASS。它不阻塞 roadmap 的下一個開發步驟：Task 4.4（spot heal / clone 的資料模型與 render 實作）仍可開始，接著 Task 4.5（spot heal 的 Mac UI）、Task 4.6（Phase 4 完整驗證輪）。

## Phase 4 Task 4.3：Mac linear gradient UI (2026-09-04, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`（自動化部分）。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，base 仍是 `main@fb7109a`，這一輪之前的 HEAD 是 `21fcda1`（Task 4.2 render composition 完成）。Product commit：`8529dc2`。開始前確認過分支/HEAD/dirty files 一致。嚴格只做 Task 4.3 範圍——第一版 Mac UI，沒有做 spot heal UI（Task 4.5）、沒有做 perspective 的完整互動 UI（本來就不在 Task 2.3 範圍內，這輪也沒碰）。
- **UI 結構**：照 `GeometryAdjustmentPanel`/`CropOverlayView` 當初建立的「Inspector 面板 + 畫布拖曳 overlay」兩層架構複製一份給線性漸層——新增 `Sources/AdjustmentUI/LocalAdjustmentsPanel.swift`（Add Gradient / 每列 enable 開關 / 刪除 / 選取中漸層的 Exposure 滑桿）跟 `Sources/LumaHarborApp/Views/LinearGradientOverlayView.swift`（每個漸層兩個獨立把手：中心點拖曳改位置、方向/範圍點拖曳同時改角度跟範圍）。`EditorToolMode` 新增 `.linearGradient`（這個 enum 自己的文件註解早就寫「未來的 local-adjustment 模式應該延伸這個 switch」，照做）；`EditorSession` 新增 `selectedLocalAdjustmentID`（純 UI 狀態，跟 `toolMode` 一樣在 `open()`/`close()` 時重置）。所有編輯都走 `editor.updateAdjustments(_:)`，跟其他每一個調整控制項共用同一條 undo/autosave 路徑。
- **拖曳數學抽成純函式，可獨立單元測試**：新增 `Sources/LumaHarborApp/Views/LinearGradientDragMath.swift`，比照 `CropDragMath` 當初從 `CropOverlayView` 抽出來的理由——方向/範圍/位置的數學邏輯不需要真的驅動一個 `DragGesture` 就能單元測試。
- **這輪抓到一個真的 bug（Task 4.1 schema 的既有缺陷）**：把拖曳數學接上 `LocalAdjustmentGeometry` 時，`testPositionDragClampsAtTheUnitSquareEdge...` 測試 RED 了——原來 Task 4.1 的 `x`/`y`/`range`/`radius`/`feather` 欄位只在 `init()` 裡 clamp，UI 直接修改既有值的欄位（`adjustments.localAdjustments[i].geometry.x = newX`，這正是這輪 UI 在做的事）完全沒有 clamp，跟 `GeometryAdjustments` 早就用 `didSet` 處理好的同一個問題。已經照 `GeometryAdjustments`/`Vignette` 的既有慣例，幫每個會被直接修改的欄位補上 `didSet` clamp 修好，RED 測試轉綠，同時重跑過 Task 4.1/4.2 的所有既有測試確認沒有回歸。
- **座標系慣例文件化在 `LinearGradientOverlayView` 自己的 header 註解裡**：SwiftUI 本身就是 y-down，所以「順時針角度」在這一層直接用 `(cos, sin)` 對應、不用像 `LocalAdjustmentRenderer`（Task 4.2，Core Image 是 y-up）那樣把 y 分量反過來——這點如果搞混，會變成 UI 上拖曳往下卻讓 render 出來的效果往上跑，已經用 `LinearGradientDragMathTests` 的方向測試（右/下/上/左四個方向各自獨立斷言）釘住。
- **在地化**：面板/overlay 新增的 11 個字串已經加進 en/zh-Hant 兩份 `.lproj`，並比照既有慣例新增 `LocalizationSmokeTest.testEveryLinearGradientStringHasAChineseTranslation`。
- **驗證**：聚焦測試（`LinearGradient*|LocalAdjustment*` + `LocalizationSmokeTest`）0 失敗。完整 `swift test`：**1559 執行**（比上一輪基準 1533 多 26）、9 skip（既有 `RawFixtureTests` 基準不變）、0 失敗。`swift build` 乾淨。iOS generic build（`AdjustmentUI`/`RawProcessingCore` 是共用 module）——`** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`，`project.pbxproj` 建置前後確認未變動。`git diff --check` 乾淨。隱私掃描零命中。
- **手動 screenshot checklist（roadmap 自己要求的 Task 4.3 交付項）**：新增 `docs/testing/beta/PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md`，13 項涵蓋新增漸層/拖曳位置把手/拖曳方向與範圍把手（含方向正確性人眼複查）/曝光滑桿/刪除/停用/多個漸層獨立性/離開編輯模式/undo/重新開啟持久化/匯出反映效果。**全部 13 項誠實列為 `NOT RUN`**——這個環境沒有真機/Simulator/人眼操作能力，這份清單就是留給真人接手用的。
- **`NOT RUN`**：spot heal UI（Task 4.5）不在這個 task 範圍內，不是遺漏。真機/Simulator 手動視覺確認也還沒做，見上面 `PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 段落。
- **Next action**：Task 4.4（Spot heal / clone model and render）——照 Task 4.1/4.2 已經打好的地基（`LocalAdjustmentKind.spotHeal`、`LocalAdjustmentGeometry` 的 `sourceX`/`sourceY`/`radius`/`healMode` 欄位都已經存在，`LocalAdjustmentRenderer` 目前明確跳過 `.spotHeal` 項目)，這輪要補上 target/source 移動測試、模式切換立即更新選取點的測試、full-resolution export 測試，實作保守的 Core Image based heal/clone，誠實記錄品質限制。或者先找時間讓真人跑一輪 `PHASE4_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 的 13 項。未經使用者明確授權，不 push、不 merge、不 rebase、不移除 worktree、不刪分支。

## Phase 4 Task 4.2：Linear gradient render composition (2026-09-04, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，base 仍是 `main@fb7109a`，這一輪之前的 HEAD 是 `a457e34`（Task 4.1 schema 完成）。Product commit：`996583b`。開始前確認過分支/HEAD/dirty files 一致。嚴格只做 Task 4.2 範圍——render composition，沒有動 Mac UI（Task 4.3）、沒有做 spot heal render（Task 4.4）、沒有碰 eyedropper/batch/preset/virtual copy（都已在更早的 Phase 完成）。
- **接進 render pipeline**：新增 `Sources/RawProcessingCore/Pipeline/LocalAdjustmentRenderer.swift`，在 `CoreImagePreviewRenderer` 跟 `PhotoExporter` 兩條路徑裡都接在 `GeometryRenderer.apply(...)` **之後**、resize/export **之前**——跟 crop 當初被獨立審查移到最後的理由一樣：使用者是在已經旋轉/裁切過的顯示畫面上拖曳漸層錨點，錨點座標必須用那個已經套用過 geometry 的座標系解讀，不能用套用 geometry 之前的來源座標系。完整的 transform order 現在統一寫在 `GeometryRenderer` 檔案自己的 header 註解裡（decode → 基本調整 → geometry → local adjustments → resize/export），作為整條 pipeline 順序的唯一權威來源。
- **Mask 與 patch 渲染邏輯**：`LocalAdjustmentRenderer.apply(_:to:)` 對每個 `isEnabled && kind == .linearGradient` 的項目,用 `CILinearGradient` 從 geometry 的 position/angle/range/feather 建出黑到白遮罩(angle 的順時針慣例跟 `GeometryRenderer.clockwiseRadians` 一致,兩個 renderer 保持一致)。`.spotHeal` 項目完全不處理,原封不動——那是 Task 4.4 的範圍。`LocalAdjustmentPatchRenderer` 重用 `AdjustmentPipeline` 處理 patch 的 7 個欄位(曝光/對比/高光/陰影/白色/黑色/飽和度,其餘欄位保持 identity 直接被跳過),`temperature`/`tint` 這兩個 `AdjustmentPipeline` 完全不處理的欄位(全域白平衡是烘進 RAW decode 本身的)則直接用 `CITemperatureAndTint` 處理——這不違反 `CoreImageRawDecoder` 自己文件裡「post-decode 溫度調整會打架」的顧慮,因為那個顧慮是針對「兩個全畫面決策打架」,局部區域調整本來就沒有對應的 RAW-level 機制,只能在 decode 之後做。
- **模型層 copy/delete/select**：`LocalAdjustment.swift` 新增 `Array<LocalAdjustment>` extension 的 `duplicating(_:)`/`removing(_:)`/`selecting(_:)`——roadmap 自己要求的「model tests for copy/delete/select」，這輪只做到模型層（純陣列操作），沒有 view model 或 UI（Task 4.3 的範圍）。
- **TDD**：先寫 `LocalAdjustmentRendererTests.swift`（synthetic 平面灰色圖，跟 `GeometryRendererTests` 的四象限標記圖不同，因為要測「這裡變亮那裡不變」需要均勻起始色，象限邊界會混淆取樣點）跟 `LocalAdjustmentTests.swift` 新增的 `LocalAdjustmentListOperationsTests`，跑 `swift test --filter` 確認真的編譯失敗才動手實作。實作完後有 2 個測試因為設計假設錯誤而 RED（`testMultipleEnabledEntriesBothApply` 誤以為兩個同方向漸層中間會沒被動到——實際上 `CILinearGradient` 過了 point1 之後會一直維持 color1，不會退回 0，這其實正是真實漸層濾鏡工具的行為，不是 bug；`testWideningFeatherSoftensTheTransition...` 用的 exposure 太強在兩種 feather 下都直接 clip 到全白，量不出差異）——都是測試設計本身的問題，修正測試後綠燈，沒有改動任何 render 邏輯本身。
- **Export 驗證用真的讀檔案 pixel，不是只看尺寸**：`PhotoExportTests.swift` 新增兩個測試——一個確認匯出檔案裡靠近漸層作用側的 pixel 真的變亮（而不是只有記憶體內的 preview 變了，export 卻悄悄跳過），一個確認 `isEnabled: false` 的項目在匯出檔案裡完全沒有作用。因為 local adjustment 不像 crop/rotate 那樣會改變輸出尺寸，尺寸相同不能證明真的套用了，必須直接讀出檔案內容比對。
- **驗證**：聚焦測試（`LocalAdjustment*|PhotoExportTests`）71 執行、0 失敗。完整 `swift test`：**1533 執行**（比上一輪基準 1513 多 20）、9 skip（既有 `RawFixtureTests` 基準不變）、0 失敗。`swift build` 乾淨。iOS generic build（`xcodebuild ... -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build`，因為 `RawProcessingCore` 是共用 module）——`** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`，`project.pbxproj` 建置前後確認未變動。`git diff --check` 乾淨。隱私掃描零命中。
- **`NOT RUN`**：Mac UI（Task 4.3）、spot heal render（Task 4.4）都不在這個 task 範圍內，不是遺漏。真機/Simulator 手動視覺確認也還沒做——這輪完全是 model/render 層的自動化測試，沒有走到 UI，等 Task 4.3 做完後才有畫面可以人眼確認漸層拖曳的實際效果。
- **Next action**：Task 4.3（Mac linear gradient UI）——crop overlay 那組的 source-contract 測試模式（`CropOverlayContractTests.swift`）已經有先例可以照抄，需要新增漸層拖曳把手的 overlay、工具模式切換、RAW non-destructive 文案，保留既有的 undo/autosave 行為。未經使用者明確授權，不 push、不 merge、不 rebase、不移除 worktree、不刪分支。

## Phase 4 Task 4.1：Local adjustment schema (2026-09-04, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，base 仍是 `main@fb7109a`，這一輪之前的 HEAD 是 `1b9476e`（Phase 3 完整驗測完成）。Product commit：`74de022`。開始前確認過分支/HEAD/dirty files 與這份檔案自己描述的狀態一致，沒有不符需要先處理。這輪嚴格只做 roadmap 的 Task 4.1 範圍——schema only，沒有動 render pipeline、Mac UI，也沒有把新欄位接進 `AdjustmentMapping`/`RenderPipeline` 任何地方。
- **資料模型**：新增 `Sources/RawProcessingCore/Model/LocalAdjustment.swift`，完全比照 roadmap 文件自己給的 sketch：`LocalAdjustment`（id/kind/isEnabled/geometry/adjustments）、`LocalAdjustmentKind`（linearGradient/spotHeal）。`LocalAdjustmentGeometry` 是單一扁平 struct（跟 `GeometryAdjustments` 既有慣例一致，不是 per-kind enum），放位置/角度/範圍/羽化/(clone 專用)來源點/半徑/heal-clone 模式——不用點陣圖遮罩（design spec §6.6 明確要求）。`LocalAdjustmentPatch` 是設計 spec §6.6 列的 9 個 mini-adjustments（曝光/對比/高光/陰影/白色/黑色/飽和度/色溫/色調，刻意沒有 vibrance），稀疏 optional 欄位，跟 `PresetCore.BasicAdjustmentPatch` 同款但獨立宣告（`RawProcessingCore` 不能依賴 `PresetCore`，依賴方向是反過來的）。`PhotoAdjustments` 新增 `localAdjustments: [LocalAdjustment]`，預設空陣列，decode 時 key 不存在就是 `[]`（沿用 Phase 2 `geometry` 欄位自己當年新增時建立的向後相容慣例）。
- **TDD**：先寫 `Tests/RawProcessingCoreTests/LocalAdjustmentTests.swift`（含 `LocalAdjustmentGeometryTests`/`LocalAdjustmentPatchTests`）跟 `PhotoAdjustmentsTests.swift` 的新測試，跑 `swift test --filter` 確認是真的編譯失敗（`cannot find 'LocalAdjustment' in scope` 等），不是只看 IDE 提示，才動手實作。實作完成、聚焦測試全綠後才 commit。
- **測試涵蓋**：backward-compatible sidecar 測試（舊 sidecar 完全沒有 `localAdjustments` key，decode 成 `[]`）、多筆 local adjustment 順序測試（JSON round trip 後順序不變）、enable/disable/delete 測試（陣列層級操作，這輪沒有 render/UI/service，schema 夠用）、geometry 的 clamp/非有限值防呆、patch 的稀疏編碼（只有真的設定過的欄位才出現在 JSON 裡）、`id`/`kind` 缺失時該拋錯而不是瞎猜的設計決定（這兩個欄位跟這個 repo 其他所有「缺欄位就退回預設值」的慣例不同，理由寫在程式碼註解裡）。
- **驗證**：聚焦測試（`LocalAdjustment*|PhotoAdjustmentsTests`）42 執行、0 失敗。完整 `swift test`：**1513 執行**（比上一輪基準 1485 多 28，全部是這輪新增的測試）、9 skip（既有 `RawFixtureTests` 基準不變）、0 失敗。`swift build` 乾淨。`git diff --check` 乾淨。隱私掃描零命中。`Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` 全程未被讀取或變動。
- **一個值得記錄的岔路（不是產品 bug）**：這輪中途真的重現過一次看起來像記憶體安全問題的 crash（`EXC_BAD_ACCESS` 在 Array buffer 存取，透過 lldb 抓到堆疊）,一度以為是新欄位設計本身有問題。深入排查後根因是：這輪中間多次 `git stash`/`stash pop` 切換去比對「有沒有我的改動」時，把 SwiftPM 的 incremental build cache 弄成不一致狀態（部分 `.o` 是舊記憶體佈局編譯的、部分是新的），造成真的 ABI 不一致當掉——`swift package clean` 後問題永久消失，跟 `LocalAdjustment` 本身的設計或程式碼完全無關。記錄在這裡是給以後的 session 一個參考：如果又在這個 worktree 遇到類似「改一個看起來很單純的欄位卻讓不相關的測試當掉」，先試 `swift package clean` 再開始深入除錯，不要浪費時間懷疑自己的程式邏輯。
- **`NOT RUN`**：render/Mac UI/eyedropper 以外的東西都不在這個 task 範圍內，不是遺漏。
- **Next action**：Task 4.2（Linear gradient render）——照 roadmap 要求先寫 synthetic image 的 failing tests（rotate/flip/crop 等的 tolerance-based assertion 慣例，這個專案 `GeometryRendererTests.swift` 已經有先例可以照抄），確認 full-resolution export 真的套用同一套 geometry 而不是只改 preview cache，文件化 transform order。未經使用者明確授權，不 push、不 merge、不 rebase、不移除 worktree、不刪分支。

## Phase 3 Mac manual verification：全部完成，20/21 PASS (2026-09-04, Claude, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`（手動驗測完整跑完）。接續上一則「A6 修復並驗證，續跑虛擬副本 D 段」段落之後，Claude 又補完了 A1（Library scope）、A7（XMP 未知欄位往返）、B1-B4（多選批次同步）、C1-C4（批次復原）、D6（Finder 外部改名/搬移），加上先前已完成的 A2-A6、D1-D5/D7，`docs/testing/beta/PHASE3_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 的 21 個必要項目現在是 **20 個 `PASS`、1 個 `NOT RUN`（B3，有明確、可驗證的理由，非阻礙）**。這一輪沒有再改動任何 product code，只有這則文件記錄本身是新 commit。分支/HEAD/base 跟上一則段落描述的狀態一致，未變動。
- **測試方法（沒有專用工具，靠 accessibility API 拼出來的）**：這個環境沒有等同 Codex CUA 的螢幕操作能力，全程用 `osascript`/System Events 的 accessibility tree（讀取按鈕/選單/滑桿位置與屬性、觸發點擊/選單動作）搭配手寫的三個 `CGEvent` 小工具（`doubleclick`——正確設置 `mouseEventClickState` 的真雙擊；`cmdclick`——**真的按住+放開 Command 鍵**而不只是在事件上標記旗標，因為 SwiftUI 的 `NSEvent.modifierFlags` 檢查讀的是即時鍵盤狀態，只標記事件旗標曾被誤判成一般點擊；`rightclick`/`drag`——真右鍵與真正的 mouseDown→多段 mouseDragged→mouseUp）拼出一個克難但確實可行的操作能力。每一項關鍵斷言都額外直接讀隔離測試環境裡的 `library.json`／sidecar JSON／`library.sqlite` 內容做交叉驗證，不只憑截圖判斷——這是在沒有專用工具時，讓證據可信度盡量貼近真人手動測試的作法。
- **A1（Library scope）現在是 `PASS`**：用「Copy to This Library」把 My Presets 的一個 preset 複製到 Library scope 後，直接讀檔確認**同一個 preset ID** 同時存在於 `~/Library/Application Support/LumaHarbor/Presets/`（My Presets）跟圖庫自己的 `.lumaharbor/presets/`（Library scope）兩個獨立位置——這比單純看 UI 篩選選單更硬的證據，證實三個 scope 真的是各自獨立儲存，不是同一份資料用不同標籤呈現。
- **A7（XMP 未知欄位往返）`PASS`**：用一份合成的 `phase3-unknown.xmp`（內含 Adobe 認得的 `crs:Exposure2012` 跟一個模擬「未來版本才有」的未知 namespace `future:MaskTree`/`future:Tags`）做完整往返：匯入（出現「已匯入」徽章）→ 匯出成 `.lhpreset` → 刪除 → 重新匯入同一份 `.lhpreset`（再次出現「已匯入」徽章）。直接讀 app 實際存放的 preset 檔案，確認 `MaskTree`／`layerName`／`portrait`／`studio` 這些未知欄位標記在完整往返後一字不差，只有認得的 `crs:Exposure2012` 被額外解析進 `patch.basic.exposure`。
- **B1/B2/B4（多選批次同步）`PASS`**：B1 確認真正的 Cmd+click（不是只標記事件旗標）能正確多選縮圖而不開啟編輯器；B2 用真正的滑鼠拖曳（不是直接寫 AXValue，因為那不會觸發批次同步倚賴的手勢開始/結束回呼）拖動來源的 Highlights 滑桿，讀 sidecar 確認目標欄位正確同步、其餘欄位不受影響；B4 確認 HSL 這類非 Basic 欄位的變更完全不會外溢到批次目標。**B3 維持 `NOT RUN`**：這個情境要求「滑鼠鍵按著拖曳滑桿」同時「Cmd+click 另一張縮圖」，任何單一滑鼠指標（真人的手或這個環境的合成指標）都做不到同時進行兩個互斥的按鍵狀態；程式碼本身用值型別快照保證這件事是結構性正確（`beginBatchGesture` 複製一份不隨後續選取變動的目標清單），且已有 `BatchAdjustmentGestureIntegrationTests` 涵蓋，不視為本輪的阻礙或缺口。

- **A6 修復驗證**：先驗證 Codex 未提交的修法（聚焦測試 71 執行 0 失敗、完整 `swift test` 1485 執行 9 skip 0 失敗、`git diff --check`/隱私掃描乾淨），照 repo 慣例 commit 為 `2d697df`。接著用 `Scripts/build-app-bundle.sh debug` 重建 app bundle、終止 Codex 留下的舊 process、以同一組 `HOME`/`CFFIXED_USER_HOME` 環境變數（指向同一個隔離測試 home `/private/tmp/LumaHarbor-Phase3-Manual-Home`）重新啟動同一個測試圖庫。用 UI scripting 重新執行一次「Restore Presets…」選同一份 `.lhpresetbackup`，截圖確認「還原完成 / 1 已存在」摘要 alert 正確跳出、按「好」可正常關閉。`docs/testing/beta/PHASE3_BUG_A6_PRESET_RESTORE_SUMMARY.md` 已標記解決，`PHASE3_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` A6 改為 `PASS`。

- **D7 的一個操作陷阱，記錄避免下次誤判**：第一次嘗試 D7 時，Claude 在**app 還活著的 process 上**直接刪除 `library.sqlite*`／`cache`，結果 app 因為手上還握著已刪除檔案的控點而跳出「無法讀取本機索引」錯誤，重新掃描後虛擬副本沒有恢復——這不是產品 bug，是操作方式錯誤（正確做法是完全 quit app 後再刪本機索引，讓下次啟動用全新的 SQLite 連線）。改成「完全 quit → 刪索引/cache → 重新啟動」的乾淨流程後，D7 正確通過（sidebar 顯示「4 張照片」，虛擬副本與 badge 都正常出現，`library.sqlite` 的 `photo` table 也確認新增了帶正確 `variant_of` 的一列）。

- **Commit**：`2d697df`（A6 修法，product + test）。這則段落與 `PHASE3_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md`/`PHASE3_BUG_A6_PRESET_RESTORE_SUMMARY.md` 的更新會在寫完後另外 commit 為 docs-only。未 push、未 merge、未 rebase、未動 worktree。
- **`NOT RUN`**：A1（Library scope 未完整覆蓋）、A7、B1-B4、C1-C4、D6 依 `PHASE3_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 詳細列出的原因維持 `NOT RUN`。

## Follow-up independent review of Phase 3 Task 3.5 (2026-09-04, Codex, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，base 仍是 `main@fb7109a4fd76035bb9ca3f492b1fa45f511a60ec`。在 Task 3.6 文件 commit `df00864` 之後，Codex 又對 `fb7109a..HEAD` 做了一次獨立審查，發現並修好一個 Task 3.5 的 sidecar 身分資料問題；product + test commit 是 `8e5caf1` (`fix: preserve virtual copy identity when editing`)。
- **Finding（真的、會破壞 portable sidecar 的虛擬副本身分，已修）**：`PhotoLibraryService.saveAdjustments(_:for:)` 每次儲存都會重建 `PhotoSidecar`，但原實作沒有帶入 `variantOf`，因此虛擬副本只要被編輯過，它自己的 sidecar 就會被靜默重寫成 `variantOf == nil`。現有的索引重建整合測試仍會通過，是因為它從 `library.json` 的 `PhotoRecord.variantOf` 恢復關係，沒有直接驗證編輯後的 sidecar；這跟 `PhotoSidecar.variantOf` 要在索引／manifest 遺失時仍能自我描述副本身分的資料契約不符。
  - **修法**：`saveAdjustments` 建立新 sidecar 時寫入 `variantOf: photo.variantOf ?? existing?.variantOf`，以當前 `PhotoAsset` 的副本身分為主，並在現有 sidecar 有身分但傳入 projection 缺少時保留已儲存值。
- **TDD**：先新增 `VirtualCopyServiceTests.testEditingAVirtualCopyPreservesItsIdentityInTheSidecar`，透過真實暫存目錄與 `FileSidecarRepository` 直接讀回編輯後的副本 sidecar。RED 確認斷言收到 `nil` 而非 original `PhotoID`；再實作上述最小修正後 GREEN。
- **驗證**：Task 3.5 聚焦組（`RelinkResolverTests|PhotoIndexMigrationTests|PhotoIndexStoreTests|VirtualCopyServiceTests|VirtualCopyLibraryViewModelTests|LibraryLifecycleTests`）74 執行、0 失敗；完整 `swift test` 1484 執行、9 skip（既有 `RawFixtureTests` fixture 相依基準）、0 失敗；`swift build` PASS；iOS generic `xcodebuild` `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`；`git diff --check` PASS；這次的 product/test 檔案隱私樣式零命中；`Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` 未變動。
- **`NOT RUN`**：`docs/testing/beta/PHASE3_MANUAL_CHECKLIST.md` 的 21 項 Mac app 人眼驗測仍全數 `NOT RUN`，其中 D2（編輯副本不影響原版）與 D7（重建索引後副本與編輯仍在）已涵蓋這條使用者流程，但尚未在實際 Mac 視窗中執行。
- **Next action**：先在真實 Mac 上跑完 Phase 3 的 21 項人眼測試；全數 PASS 後再由使用者決定是否 merge 到 `main`。未經使用者明確授權，不 push、不 merge、不 rebase、不移除 worktree、不刪分支。

## Independent review of Phase 3 Task 3.5 (2026-09-04, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **狀態**：`DONE`（修 bug 的回合，修掉這次審查發現的兩個真實、可從 UI 或既有支援流程直接觸發的 bug）。分支 `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`，修 bug 的 commit `57cb884`，在 Task 3.5 自己的文件 commit `e2945cd` 之上再加一個。base 分支 `main` 在 `fb7109a4fd76035bb9ca3f492b1fa45f511a60ec`，未變動。開始前先確認過分支／HEAD／dirty files 與 `CURRENT.md` 本身 Task 3.5 段落的描述一致（HEAD `e2945cd`，base `fb7109a`，4 個 product+test commit `3185313`/`0be541c`/`8ad0190`/`66edf43`）——相符，沒有不一致需要先停下來處理。
- **審查方式**：完整讀過 `docs/coordination/CURRENT.md`（全文，含前面每一輪「Independent review of Phase 3 Task 3.x」段落，作為這次審查深度與寫法的範本）、`docs/coordination/DECISIONS.md`、`AGENTS.md`、roadmap 與 design spec 裡 Task 3.5／虛擬副本（§6.10）的段落。逐一 `git show` 4 個 product commit 的完整 diff，並讀過每個被改到的檔案（不只 diff hunk）本身，確認 scan／relink／sidecar 讀寫／undo／autosave／preset apply／batch sync／export 這些沒有在 Task 3.5 範圍內的既有流程，是「真的沒受影響」而不是「沒被檢查」——用 `rg -rln variantOf` 之類的方式確認 batch sync／preset apply／export／search 完全沒碰過 `variantOf`，跟 CURRENT.md 宣稱的一致。
- **Finding 1（真的、會靜默遺失使用者資料，已修）——虛擬副本撐不過本機索引重建**：`PhotoLibraryService.performScan` 只會替「掃描時實際找到的檔案」建立 index row；虛擬副本因為跟原始照片共用同一個 `relativePath`，掃描時永遠不會被獨立找到，而且從 commit `3185313` 起 `removePhotos(inLibrary:notSeenSince:)` 又無條件排除所有 `variant_of IS NOT NULL` 的 row 不被清掃。兩者疊加的結果：`PhotoLibraryService.resetRebuildableLocalData()`——這支專案自己文件裡明寫「Rebuilding a deleted SQLite database is the same code path: the manifest on the SSD supplies the PhotoIDs, so photos keep their edits (spec §13.9)」、也有既有整合測試 `LibraryLifecycleTests.testDeletingTheLocalIndexAndCacheStillRebuildsFromTheDrive` 鎖住這個保證——對虛擬副本其實是假的：只要本機 SQLite 索引被刪除重建（這是這個 app 自己文件化、給使用者的正式復原路徑，不是什麼冷門手法），該圖庫底下每一個虛擬副本都會從畫面上永久消失，即使它的 sidecar 與 `library.json` 裡的紀錄都還好端端留在硬碟上，成為孤兒資料。這不是一次刻意的 scope 邊界（Task 3.5 自己記錄的三個 scope 邊界都沒提到這件事），是這一輪真的漏掉的東西。
  - **修法**：`performScan` 在每次成功重新掃描到某張原始照片時，都會把 manifest 裡記錄「複製自這張照片」的虛擬副本 record 一併還原回 index（新函式 `virtualCopyAssets(for:...)`，遞迴走訪，讓「副本的副本」這種鏈也能一起回來）。副本自己的 `relativePath`／`fingerprint` 一律取自它自己凍結的 `PhotoRecord`（絕不是原始照片目前、可能已經被搬移過的路徑），`metadata`／`status` 則沿用剛剛重新掃描到的原始照片本身的值（兩者本來就是同一份 RAW 檔案）。如果某筆副本 record 已經讀不到對應的 sidecar，就直接跳過不還原——因為讓一張副本「真的存在」的是它自己的 sidecar，不是 manifest 裡的紀錄；`deleteVirtualCopy(_:)` 刪除時是先刪 sidecar（正確性關卡），manifest 清理只是盡力而為，如果剛好失敗，不能讓下一次重新掃描把已刪除的副本從殘留的 manifest 紀錄裡復活回來。
- **Finding 2（真的、會讓資料在畫面上直接消失，已修）——副本的副本會從圖庫網格裡整個不見**：`LibraryViewModel.orderedForDisplay` 把每個虛擬副本依 `variantOf` 分組掛在自己原始照片後面，但原本的實作只往下展開一層——一個副本自己底下如果又有副本（`createVirtualCopy(of:)` 自己的文件明講支援「對副本再建副本」，`LibraryGridView` 的右鍵選單「Duplicate as Virtual Copy」也對任何照片一律提供，使用者從畫面上就能直接做到這件事），那個第二代副本會留在中間字典裡，永遠沒有被接回最終的 `result` 陣列，整張照片就從網格裡消失，不是排序錯誤，是真的看不到、選不到。
  - **修法**：把 `orderedForDisplay` 改成遞迴展開——把每個節點自己的分組成員，連同它們自己底下的分組，一起接進 `result`，讓副本鏈不論幾代都會出現在畫面上。
- **這次審查也確認沒問題、不是 bug 的部分**：schema migration 的版本閘門（v1→v2、v2→v3 各自獨立判斷 `version < 2`／`version < 3`，重跑不會撞到「欄位已存在」；全新空資料庫會在同一次 init 裡一路做到 v3，不需要先過 v2）；`RelinkResolver.resolve` 的兩個分支都正確排除 `variantOf != nil` 的 record；`createVirtualCopy` 的錯誤處理順序（sidecar 寫入是正確性關卡，會 throw；manifest 更新是 best-effort，跟其他掃描流程對唯讀硬碟的容忍度一致）；`deleteVirtualCopy` 對「不是虛擬副本」的輸入會正確 refuse；本地化：`en.lproj`／`zh-Hant.lproj` 各 460 個 key，一一對應無缺漏。
- **TDD**：兩個 Finding 都先用一個會真的失敗的整合測試確認 RED，再實作修法確認 GREEN。Finding 1：`LibraryLifecycleTests.testDeletingTheLocalIndexAndCacheStillRebuildsAVirtualCopyFromTheDrive`（先失敗：重建後只剩原始照片，副本連同它自己的獨立調整值一起消失），修完後通過。Finding 2：`VirtualCopyLibraryViewModelTests.testACopyOfAVirtualCopyStillAppearsInTheGrid`（先失敗：`model.photos.count` 只有 2，第二代副本完全找不到），修完後通過。額外新增第三個測試 `VirtualCopyServiceTests.testRescanNeverResurrectsACopyWhoseSidecarIsGoneButWhoseManifestRecordLingers`，鎖住 Finding 1 修法本身可能引入的新風險（複製一份 delete 沒清乾淨的 manifest 殘留紀錄，驗證重新掃描不會把已刪除的副本復活）——先手動註解掉修法裡的 sidecar 存在性檢查確認這個測試會真的失敗，再復原確認通過。另外也用這個 repo 既有的作法（把某個檔案暫時還原成上一個 commit 前的內容、跑測試、再復原），對這一輪本來就有的三個測試做了抽查，全部在還原對應 product 程式碼後如預期失敗：`RelinkResolverTests.testPathMatchIgnoresAVirtualCopyRecordAtTheSamePath`／`testFingerprintFallbackNeverTreatsAVirtualCopyAsAMoveCandidate`、`PhotoIndexStoreTests.testSweepingNeverRemovesAVirtualCopyRegardlessOfItsOwnLastSeenAt`、`VirtualCopyServiceTests.testDeletingTheOriginalPhotoThroughThisAPIIsRefused`——都是有鑑別力、不是恰好通過的測試。
- **新增的測試**：`LibraryLifecycleTests` +1、`VirtualCopyLibraryViewModelTests` +1、`VirtualCopyServiceTests` +1。
- **驗證**：完整 `swift test` —— 1483 個測試執行（比 Task 3.5 自己回合結束時的 1480 多 3 個），9 個 skip（`RawFixtureTests` 既有的 fixture 相依基準，沒變），0 個失敗。`swift build` 乾淨。`xcodebuild -project Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj -scheme LumaHarborPad -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build` —— `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`。`git diff --check`（工作目錄與 `fb7109a..57cb884` 整個範圍）都乾淨。隱私掃描（`rg -n "/Users/|/Volumes/|/private/|<TEAM_ID>|teamIdentifier:|DEVELOPMENT_TEAM"`）：對 `fb7109a..57cb884` 整段 diff 跑過，唯一命中的都是 `CURRENT.md`／`docs/testing/reports/` 自己文字裡提到這個 worktree本身已公開的路徑，跟前面每一輪的既有慣例一致，沒有簽章／裝置專屬樣式命中；對我自己這次修 bug 的 diff 單獨跑過，零命中。`Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` 確認在 iOS build 前後都沒有變動。
- **`NOT RUN`**：真機／Simulator／人眼手動檢查（這個環境沒有辦法啟動並操作 Mac app 的畫面）——虛擬副本 badge 的實際外觀、右鍵選單的真實觸感、以及「刪除索引重建」在真實視窗上是否如預期把副本找回來，都只能靠這次新增與既有的單元／整合測試驗證，沒有人在跑起來的視窗前確認過。跟先前每一輪 UI／資料流程的 `NOT RUN` 記錄狀況一致。
- **Next action**：Task 3.6（Phase 3 verification report／handoff——每個 task 的重點測試、`swift test`、`git diff --check`、隱私掃描、Mac 手動檢查清單涵蓋 preset apply／multi-select sync／batch undo／virtual copy persistence）。Task 3.5 本身的獨立審查到此已完成並修好找到的問題，不再是進入 Task 3.6 的阻礙。未經使用者明確授權，不 push、不 merge、不 rebase、不移除 worktree、不刪分支。

## Phase 3 Task 3.5 (2026-09-03/04, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch, 4 commits)

- **Status**: `DONE`. Branch `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`. Base branch `main` at `fb7109a4fd76035bb9ca3f492b1fa45f511a60ec`, unchanged. Four product+test commits, one per roadmap bullet, each independently verified:
  1. `3185313` -- identity model + schema (sidecar/schema tests for original vs copy identity).
  2. `0be541c` -- `PhotoLibraryService.createVirtualCopy`/`deleteVirtualCopy` (delete-safety tests).
  3. `8ad0190` -- library display grouping + `LibraryViewModel` duplicate/delete wiring (adjacency query tests).
  4. `66edf43` -- grid badge/name/context-menu actions (UI).
- **Scope**: Phase 3 roadmap Task 3.5 ("Virtual copy" — sidecar/schema tests for original vs copy identity; library query tests that variants appear adjacent to original; delete tests: deleting virtual copy never deletes RAW original or other copies; UI badge/name/duplicate/delete copy actions). Scoped, by explicit user decision before starting (`AskUserQuestion`), to exactly those four bullets — batch sync, preset apply, export and search are **not** extended to cover virtual copies this round; that's tracked as a deliberate follow-up, not an oversight.
- **Data model decision (also by explicit user choice, offered as two options)**: extended the existing `PhotoAsset` with `variantOf: PhotoID?`/`variantName: String?` rather than introducing the roadmap doc's own separate `PhotoVariant` struct sketch. A virtual copy is a distinct `PhotoID` (and therefore its own independent sidecar/adjustments, reusing every existing load/save/editor/export/thumbnail code path unchanged) sharing an original's `relativePath`/`fingerprint` — the same underlying RAW file, never duplicated on disk.
- **What changed**:
  - `PhotoAsset.variantOf`/`.variantName` (new); mirrored onto `PhotoRecord` (`library.json`) and `PhotoSidecar.variantOf` (the sidecar), following the existing "sidecar embeds a redundant copy of its own identifying info" convention (`sourceRelativePath`/`sourceFingerprint`) — all additive, decode to `nil` on older data.
  - `RelinkResolver.resolve` now filters both its path-match and fingerprint-fallback branches to `variantOf == nil` records only: a scanned file always resolves identity against the original, never a copy sharing its path/fingerprint, and a copy never itself surfaces as an ambiguous move candidate.
  - `PhotoIndexStore` schema v2 -> v3: nullable `variant_of`/`variant_name` columns. The migration is now version-gated *per step* (v1->v2 statements only run below v2, v3's only below v3) rather than one unconditional block — re-running the v1->v2 `ALTER TABLE`s against an already-v2 database (the real upgrade path for every existing installation) would fail with "duplicate column name". `removePhotos(inLibrary:notSeenSince:)` now excludes `variant_of IS NOT NULL` rows unconditionally: a virtual copy is never independently rediscovered by a folder scan (it shares its original's path), so nothing ever refreshes its own `last_seen_at`, and without this exemption every copy would look stale and be swept on the very next scan.
  - `PhotoLibraryService.createVirtualCopy(of:named:)` mints a fresh `PhotoID`, copies the source's `relativePath`/`fingerprint`/`metadata`, and starts from a copy of the source's own *current* adjustments (ordinary "duplicate" semantics — identical now, independent from this point on). Works against either an original or another copy; `variantOf` always points directly at whatever was duplicated, never chased to some "root" (this scope has no nested copy trees). Sidecar write is the correctness gate (throws on failure); the manifest update is best-effort, matching every scan's own tolerance for a read-only drive leaving `library.json` briefly stale.
  - `PhotoLibraryService.deleteVirtualCopy(_:)` removes only that copy's own sidecar and index row — never the shared RAW file, or the manifest record/sidecar/index row of the original or any other copy. Refuses (`LibraryError.notAVirtualCopy`) anything that isn't itself a virtual copy.
  - `LibraryViewModel.orderedForDisplay` groups every virtual copy immediately after its own original **by `variantOf`'s `PhotoID`, not by relativePath**. A copy shares its original's `relativePath`/`captureDate` only at the *moment* it's created, so the plain `displayOrder` comparator already ties them together in the common case — this pass stops being a no-op once the original is later renamed/moved and relinked to a new path (a virtual copy's own record is never touched by scanning, so its `relativePath` stays frozen at whatever it was when duplicated). `duplicateAsVirtualCopy(_:named:)`/`deleteVirtualCopy(_:)` wire the service calls into the view model: delete is attempted *before* touching any editor/selection state (a failure never tears down an in-progress edit for nothing), and the editor only closes once a delete of its own open photo has actually succeeded.
  - `PhotoGridCell` shows a `doc.on.doc.fill` badge (bottom-trailing, its own corner) for any `photo.isVirtualCopy`, and displays `variantName ?? filename`. `LibraryGridView`'s new per-cell context menu offers "Duplicate as Virtual Copy" unconditionally and "Delete Virtual Copy" only when the photo actually is one.
  - New localized strings (badge tooltip, context menu items, error alerts) landed in both `en.lproj` and `zh-Hant.lproj`.
- **TDD, including one caught-and-corrected test design mistake worth flagging**: the first draft of the "variants appear adjacent to original" test used a same-session copy, which passed with the grouping code *removed* too — the copy's `relativePath`/`captureDate` are identical to its original's at creation time, so the plain `displayOrder` comparator's own tie-break already achieves adjacency in that case, making the test pass for the wrong reason (an emergent property of metadata-copying, not of the grouping code being tested). Caught by deliberately reverting the grouping code and re-running before trusting the green result (this repo's established practice, not a one-off) — the temp-revert-and-rerun revealed the test didn't discriminate. Redesigned around the scenario that actually requires ID-based grouping: the original renamed/moved and relinked to a new path after a copy already exists, at which point the copy's frozen path drifts from the original's new one. Re-verified: temp-reverting grouping now fails exactly and only that new test, confirming it's a real, load-bearing test.
- **Tests added**: `RelinkResolverTests` +3. `PhotoIndexMigrationTests` +2 (plus updated 3 pre-existing hardcoded-schema-version assertions from `2` to `3`). `PhotoIndexStoreTests` +3. `VirtualCopyServiceTests` (new file, 7). `VirtualCopyLibraryViewModelTests` (new file, 5). `LibraryGridMultiSelectContractTests` +2. `LocalizationSmokeTest` +1 method (6 keys).
- **Verification** (run after each of the 4 commits, not just at the end): full `swift test` — 1480 executed (up from 1457 before this task), 9 skipped (unchanged `RawFixtureTests` fixture-dependent baseline), 0 failures at every step. `git diff --check` — clean at every step. Privacy scan (every changed/new file individually) — no hits at every step. `swift build` — clean at every step. `xcodebuild -project Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj -scheme LumaHarborPad -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build` — `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **` at every step. `Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` confirmed untouched before and after every step.
- **`NOT RUN`**: every real-device/Simulator/human-eyes manual check — this environment cannot launch and drive the Mac app's UI, so the badge's actual on-screen appearance, the context menu's real feel, and a real rename-then-rescan producing the expected grid order are only verified via unit/integration tests, never a human at a running window. Consistent with every prior Phase 2/3 UI round's own `NOT RUN` entry in this environment.
- **Deliberate, documented scope boundaries — not omissions**: (1) batch sync (Task 3.3/3.4), preset apply/export, and search/filter do not yet know about virtual copies at all — a copy behaves as an ordinary, independent `PhotoAsset` everywhere except the grid's own grouping and the two new context-menu actions. (2) No "delete the original photo" operation exists or is planned by this task — `deleteVirtualCopy` explicitly refuses anything that isn't itself a copy. (3) No nested-copy-tree UI or semantics — a copy of a copy is just another flat copy, `variantOf` pointing at whatever was actually duplicated.
- **Independent review**: performed — see "Independent review of Phase 3 Task 3.5" above. Found and fixed two real bugs (a virtual copy silently failing to survive `resetRebuildableLocalData()` + rescan, and a copy of a copy silently vanishing from the grid).
- **Next action**: Task 3.6 (Phase 3 verification report — focused tests per task, `swift test`, `git diff --check`, privacy scan, manual Mac checklist for preset apply/multi-select sync/batch undo/virtual copy persistence). Do not push, merge, rebase, remove this worktree, or commit any signing setting without explicit user authorization.

## Independent review of the Task 3.4 follow-up round (2026-09-03, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **Status**: `DONE` (fix-only round for the review's one real finding, plus a documentation tightening for its one real-but-minor observation). Branch `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`, product+test commit `6752d21`, one commit ahead of the follow-up round's own docs commit `7d8600b`. Base branch `main` at `fb7109a4fd76035bb9ca3f492b1fa45f511a60ec`, unchanged.
- **Review performed by**: a fresh general-purpose subagent with no prior context on this work, reviewing `git diff f1de4ff..HEAD -- Sources/ Tests/` (the follow-up round's own diff, i.e. Findings 2 and 4's fixes) plus `CURRENT.md`'s matching entry, explicitly told to verify rather than accept any claim at face value — including writing and running small standalone Swift snippets to confirm basic language semantics (e.g. `defer`'s exact timing inside a `for` loop) rather than reasoning from memory.
- **Finding 1 — real, fixed, a regression introduced by the very same round's own Finding 4 fix**: `undoLastBatchTransaction()` read `lastBatchTransaction` into a local `transaction`, awaited `batchSyncService.undo(transaction)`, then wrote back to `lastBatchTransaction` based on the outcome — with no check that `lastBatchTransaction` was still the same transaction it started with. Concrete scenario: undo of T1 (targets B) is in flight; while it's suspended mid-write, an entirely unrelated batch sync (T2, targets D) completes normally via `endBatchGesture`, replacing `lastBatchTransaction` with T2. When T1's undo then finishes: if it fully succeeded, the old code's `lastBatchTransaction = nil` wiped out T2's own, completely untouched undo availability; if T1 partially failed, T2 was left looking like a partially-failed transaction available for retry — either way, silently corrupting the state of a sync the user never asked to touch. This was invisible to both of Finding 4's own new tests, since neither drove a second, unrelated `endBatchGesture` while an undo was in flight.
  - **Fix**: only writes back to `lastBatchTransaction` when it's still the exact transaction (compared by `id`, `BatchAdjustmentTransaction` already being `Equatable` with a `UUID`) this call started with; a newer transaction that landed in the meantime is left untouched.
- **Finding 2 — real-but-minor, documentation only, not a behavior change**: `undo(_:)`'s `targetsInFlight` conflict branch always tallies a target as `failed`, but it never got far enough to load and compare that target against `syncedPatch` — a retry, once the conflicting operation clears, might correctly re-tally the same target as `skipped` instead (nothing was actually left to fix). Not worth a new `BatchUndoSummary` category for a self-correcting-on-retry display nuance that never risks data safety; tightened the doc comment on `undo(_:)` to say so plainly instead of overclaiming precision.
- **Confirmed correct, not bugs (verified, not assumed)**: the reviewer independently re-derived and confirmed three things this round's own commit message had claimed: (1) `defer` inside a `for` loop fires at the end of each iteration, not at function return — checked with standalone Swift snippets, so `targetsInFlight`'s insert/defer-remove pairing in both `commitGesture` and `undo` is correct and doesn't leak entries across iterations within one call. (2) `isUndoingLastBatchTransaction`'s `defer` is scoped to the whole function, so it stays `true` through the badge-refresh loop too, not just the `batchSyncService.undo(_:)` await — confirmed via the existing `testUndoLastBatchTransactionIgnoresASecondCallWhileTheFirstIsStillRunning` actually being blocked by the flag itself, not by `lastBatchTransaction` coincidentally already being `nil`. (3) Both new gate/rendezvous-based concurrency tests rely on `CheckedContinuation`'s real happens-before semantics, not on Swift's scheduler "usually" behaving a certain way, so neither is expected to be flaky on other machines or toolchain versions.
- **TDD**: RED confirmed for Finding 1's fix via `testUndoLastBatchTransactionDoesNotClobberAnUnrelatedTransactionThatLandsWhileItIsInFlight` (new, `BatchAdjustmentGestureIntegrationTests`) — a gate pauses T1's own revert-write to B mid-flight, an unrelated T2 (source C → target D) is driven to completion while T1's undo is still suspended there, then T1's undo is released. Failed against the pre-fix code exactly as predicted (`lastBatchTransaction` came back `nil` instead of T2's own id). The test's first draft was itself briefly wrong — its wait condition polled a raw save count, which a source photo's own flush-on-navigate-away autosave could satisfy before T2's own target save actually landed, giving a false "T2 already landed" reading; fixed to wait on T2's own target's save specifically before restoring the production fix and confirming both RED (without the fix) and GREEN (with it).
- **Tests added**: `BatchAdjustmentGestureIntegrationTests` +1.
- **Verification**: full `swift test` — 1457 executed (up from 1456), 9 skipped (unchanged `RawFixtureTests` fixture-dependent baseline), 0 failures. `git diff --check` — clean. Privacy scan (all 3 changed files individually) — no hits. `swift build` — clean. `xcodebuild -project Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj -scheme LumaHarborPad -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build` — `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`. `Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` confirmed untouched before and after.
- **`NOT RUN`**: real-device/Simulator/human-eyes manual check of the fixed concurrent-transaction behavior — same standing limitation as every prior round in this environment.
- **Remaining known follow-up (unchanged, not addressed this round)**: Finding 3 from the original Task 3.4 independent review (below) — "Undo Batch Sync" is one-shot by design, and its own menu text still doesn't say *which* sync it will revert when several have happened in a row.
- **Next action**: Task 3.5 (virtual copy) or Task 3.6 (Phase 3 verification report). Given three consecutive rounds of independent review on this same batch-undo feature have each found and fixed one real bug (including this round's review catching a regression the *previous* review's own fix introduced), a further review of Task 3.5 before moving to 3.6 remains this repo's established practice, not optional polish. Do not push, merge, rebase, remove this worktree, or commit any signing setting without explicit user authorization.

## Phase 3 Task 3.4 follow-up round: Findings 2 and 4 (2026-09-03, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **Status**: `DONE`. Picked up, by explicit user request, the two items the Task 3.4 independent review below had left as tracked-but-unfixed follow-ups. Branch `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`, product+test commit `b044a62`, one commit ahead of the Task 3.4 independent-review docs commit `f1de4ff`. Base branch `main` at `fb7109a4fd76035bb9ca3f492b1fa45f511a60ec`, unchanged.
- **Finding 2, now fixed — lost-update race between a concurrent `commitGesture` and `undo` sharing a target**: `BatchAdjustmentSyncService.commitGesture`/`undo` each run a target's own `load → merge → save` cycle across several `await` points; actor isolation only guarantees one call's *unsuspended* code runs at a time, not that a whole such cycle is atomic against a second call into the same actor that starts while the first is suspended. Concrete scenario: a new drag's `commitGesture` is still mid-flight (awaiting a slow write) for target B when the user chooses "Undo Batch Sync" for an earlier transaction that also targets B — `undo`'s own load could read the pre-drag value and its later save could land *after* the drag's own save, silently erasing the drag's result.
  - **Fix**: a new `targetsInFlight: Set<PhotoID>` in the actor. Both `commitGesture` and `undo` register a target for the duration of its own cycle; a target already registered by the other call is left alone (tallied `.failure`/`failed`) instead of racing into it.
  - **Verified with a deterministic test, not a timing-dependent one**: `testUndoDoesNotRaceAConcurrentCommitGestureOnTheSameTarget` uses a rendezvous (`Interleaver`) to pause a second, in-flight sync's own load for the shared target, drives `undo` to completion while it's paused there, then releases it — proving `undo` backs off and the in-flight sync's own result survives. Confirmed this test actually detects the bug: temporarily removing the `targetsInFlight` guard reproduced a hang (both operations contending for the same test rendezvous, since nothing serializes them), and restoring the guard made it pass in ~3ms.
- **Finding 4, now fixed — a partially-failed undo permanently lost its own retry path**: `LibraryViewModel.undoLastBatchTransaction()` discarded `lastBatchTransaction` unconditionally, even when `BatchUndoSummary.failed > 0` — once discarded, there was no way to retry just the target(s) that failed to revert short of a manual edit or a fresh, reversed batch sync.
  - **Fix**: `undoLastBatchTransaction()` now only clears `lastBatchTransaction` when `summary.failed == 0`. A partial failure keeps it around so choosing "Undo Batch Sync" again retries — safely: the earlier independent-review fix's own conflict check means a target already reverted on the first attempt no longer matches its recorded `after` value, so the retry `skipped`s it rather than reverting it a second time.
  - **Collateral fix, not scope creep**: making the transaction retryable reopened a *different* race the old unconditional-clear-before-await had accidentally been preventing — two rapid calls to `undoLastBatchTransaction()` (e.g. a double-click before the menu item re-disables) could now both read the same still-present transaction and start their own concurrent revert. Added `isUndoingLastBatchTransaction`, a plain `Bool` guard checked and set synchronously before the first `await`, so a second call while the first is running is ignored outright. Verified the same way: removing just this guard (keeping the retry fix) reproduced the second call actually running and returning a spurious `failed: 1` from racing the actor-level `targetsInFlight` guard; restoring it made the second call return `nil` as intended.
- **TDD**: RED confirmed for both fixes, each verified by a manual temporary-revert-and-rerun (not just "test passes with the fix present") — see the two "Verified..." notes above. `BatchAdjustmentSyncServiceTests` +1 (`testUndoDoesNotRaceAConcurrentCommitGestureOnTheSameTarget`). `BatchAdjustmentGestureIntegrationTests` +2 (`testUndoLastBatchTransactionKeepsTheTransactionForRetryAfterAPartialFailure`, `testUndoLastBatchTransactionIgnoresASecondCallWhileTheFirstIsStillRunning`).
- **Tests added**: `BatchAdjustmentSyncServiceTests` +1. `BatchAdjustmentGestureIntegrationTests` +2.
- **Verification**: full `swift test` — 1456 executed (up from 1453), 9 skipped (unchanged `RawFixtureTests` fixture-dependent baseline), 0 failures. `git diff --check` — clean. Privacy scan (all 4 changed files individually) — no hits. `swift build` — clean. `xcodebuild -project Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj -scheme LumaHarborPad -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build` — `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`. `Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` confirmed untouched before and after.
- **`NOT RUN`**: real-device/Simulator/human-eyes manual check of the fixed retry/concurrency behavior — same standing limitation as every prior round in this environment.
- **Remaining known follow-up (unchanged, not addressed this round)**: Finding 3 from the Task 3.4 independent review below — "Undo Batch Sync" is one-shot by design, and its own menu text still doesn't say *which* sync it will revert when several have happened in a row. A real, minor discoverability gap; a regression test already locks in the underlying one-shot behavior as intentional.
- **Independent review**: performed — see "Independent review of the Task 3.4 follow-up round" above. Found one real regression this very round's own Finding 4 fix had introduced (fixed) and one real-but-minor reporting-precision observation (documentation only).
- **Next action**: Task 3.5 (virtual copy) or Task 3.6 (Phase 3 verification report). Do not push, merge, rebase, remove this worktree, or commit any signing setting without explicit user authorization.

## Independent review of Phase 3 Task 3.4 (2026-09-03, Claude, TDD, in this worktree/branch)

- **Status**: `DONE` (fix-only round for the review's one real, silent-data-loss finding, plus one cheap wording fix and one regression test locking in an already-documented behavior). Branch `claude/awayphotoraweditor-parity-phase2-geometry`, product+test commit `d1e9236`, one commit ahead of Task 3.4's docs commit `024a913`. Base branch `main` at `fb7109a4fd76035bb9ca3f492b1fa45f511a60ec`, unchanged.
- **Review performed by**: a fresh general-purpose subagent with no prior context on this work, reviewing `git diff a785205..HEAD -- Sources/ Tests/` (Task 3.4's own diff) plus `CURRENT.md`'s Task 3.4 entry, explicitly told to verify rather than accept any "documented scope boundary" claim, following the same pattern every prior round's independent review has used.
- **Finding 1 — real, silent data loss, fixed**: `BatchAdjustmentSyncService.undo(_:)` merged a target's `before` patch back onto its *current* adjustments unconditionally, with no check that the synced field still held what the sync actually wrote. Concrete scenario: batch-sync exposure 0→1.5 onto target B, then open B directly and edit its exposure to 2.5 (a deliberate, unrelated-to-the-batch edit) before choosing "Undo Batch Sync" — B's exposure was silently overwritten back to 0, permanently discarding the 2.5 edit with no warning. The method's own doc comment claimed "anything a target picked up after the original sync... survives the undo" — true only for fields the sync never touched, not for the synced field itself, which is exactly the field most likely to matter here.
  - **Fix**: `undo(_:)` now compares the target's current value on exactly `modifiedFieldIDs` against `after[target]` (what the sync itself wrote) before reverting; a mismatch means the target moved on since (edited directly, or by a later, unrelated batch sync) — that target is left alone entirely and tallied as `skipped` (its meaning widened accordingly: "nothing safe to revert," whether because the original sync never wrote to it or because it's since diverged), rather than guessing which part is still safe to overwrite.
- **Finding 5 — real-but-minor, fixed (cheap, concrete)**: the batch-undo report copy reused `PresetRestoreSummary`'s existing "failed" key; its zh-Hant translation ("失敗") has no photo-counting measure word, inconsistent with "reverted"/"skipped" (both "張…") once joined into one sentence — "2 張已還原, 1 失敗, 1 張跳過" reads unevenly. Gave batch-undo's own "failed to revert" key ("張復原失敗") instead, leaving `PresetRestoreSummary`'s own usage of "failed" untouched.
- **Findings 2–4 — real-but-minor or unconfirmed, NOT fixed this round, tracked here as known follow-ups.** *(Update: Findings 2 and 4 were fixed in the very next round — see "Phase 3 Task 3.4 follow-up round: Findings 2 and 4" above. Finding 3 remains open, addressed only by a regression test.)*
  2. ~~**Suspected, unconfirmed**: `BatchAdjustmentSyncService` is an `actor`, but `undo(_:)`'s per-target `load → apply → save` isn't atomic across its own `await` points~~ — confirmed real and fixed; see the follow-up round above.
  3. `LibraryViewModel.lastBatchTransaction` is one-shot by design (documented in Task 3.4's own entry above) — two consecutive syncs leave only the second undoable, and the first's own sync results on its targets are permanent unless manually reverted. Now locked in by a new regression test (`testUndoLastBatchTransactionOnlyRevertsTheMostRecentOfTwoConsecutiveSyncs`) confirming this is deliberate, tested behavior rather than an unverified claim — the "Undo Batch Sync" menu item's own text still doesn't say which sync it will revert, which remains a real (if minor) discoverability gap for a user who did several batch edits in a row.
  4. ~~If `undo(_:)`'s own revert write fails for a target (tallied as `failed`), `LibraryViewModel.undoLastBatchTransaction()` unconditionally discards `lastBatchTransaction` anyway~~ — fixed; see the follow-up round above.
- **TDD**: RED confirmed for Finding 1's fix. `BatchAdjustmentSyncServiceTests` +2: `testUndoDoesNotOverwriteAFieldTheTargetHasBeenEditedOnSinceTheSync` (failed against the pre-fix `undo(_:)` — the target's later manual edit was overwritten) and `testUndoStillRevertsWhenTheTargetWasOnlyEditedOnAnUnrelatedFieldSinceTheSync` (a regression guard, passed immediately, confirming the new conflict check is scoped to the synced fields only — an unrelated later edit still doesn't block a normal revert). `BatchAdjustmentGestureIntegrationTests` +1 (`testUndoLastBatchTransactionOnlyRevertsTheMostRecentOfTwoConsecutiveSyncs`, Finding 3's regression guard, end to end through `LibraryViewModel`) — passed immediately, confirming the one-shot behavior already worked as documented; this test exists to keep it that way, not because it was broken.
- **Tests added**: `BatchAdjustmentSyncServiceTests` +2. `BatchAdjustmentGestureIntegrationTests` +1.
- **Verification**: full `swift test` — 1453 executed (up from 1450), 9 skipped (unchanged `RawFixtureTests` fixture-dependent baseline), 0 failures. `git diff --check` — clean. Privacy scan (all 7 changed files individually) — no hits. `swift build` — clean. `xcodebuild -project Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj -scheme LumaHarborPad -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO build` — `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`. `Apps/LumaHarborPad.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` confirmed untouched before and after.
